                    <tr>
                        <td style="padding: 28px 36px 18px 36px;">
                            <div style="font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; color: #0F172A; margin-bottom: 14px;">
                                💡 AI Executive Summary: Key News Insights
                            </div>
                            <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" width="100%" style="background-color: #F8FAFC; border-left: 4px solid #FF9900; border: 1px solid #E2E8F0; border-left-width: 4px;">
                                <tr>
                                    <td style="padding: 20px 24px; font-size: 13.5px; color: #334155; line-height: 1.7em; text-align: justify;">
                                        글로벌 빅테크의 AI 인프라 투자가 차세대 가속기 클라우드 확보와 아시아 거점 하이퍼스케일 캠퍼스 확장을 축으로 재편되는 양상임. 코어위브가 허드슨리버트레이딩과 수십억 달러 규모 계약을 체결하고 엔비디아 차세대 '베라 루빈 NVL72' 기반 연구 플랫폼 공급에 착수함 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=CoreWeave And Hudson River Trading Sign Multi-Billion-Dollar AI Cloud Deal For NVIDIA Vera Rubin Research Platform&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[1]</a> <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Hudson River Trading to Build Next-Gen Research Platform Powered by NVIDIA Vera Rubin NVL72 on CoreWeave Cloud&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[2]</a>. 메타 역시 마이크로소프트 애저에 수억 달러를 투입하며 핵심 고객사로 부상하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMitAFBVV95cUxQa0ZRNFctZTBxQUpIVXJ1T1dDS1FsUUhpLXl4RFZGTUZZVjA2aTIyZTdkQndhWElmVlBpb1d1MHZ6LWpDU2RscDJCRkY3Nmd5UDdyUFpuY2VNNVlvV1lWdWlXeFYwVmViX2xOZlpabU1vNmVfRS1wU2FLV2xzUTJGNThQbDVJVTlwZ2pPbUk5R01rQ0Npemw5cTBpRG5TSzBYWGt6bV9GTTZUd3VxMDdrWXdtem0?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[13]</a>, 말레이시아 YTL 파워는 JLand와 조호르에 GW급 캠퍼스를 조성해 2030년까지 데이터센터 용량을 2.4GW로 확대하기로 합의함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiqgFBVV95cUxQLWZOdlN2aXhoMXR4U3hlakVqRVowMUs2Y3dWdUZ4bkZPQ0VvNm5fdzA1bjJaNWhJazRnYW1IQkVJb0lMZEdNU3Ffa1E2M0JJWFNwRzhYZHczM2kyODFyOWktQmVtejY0bER2TVJlUEt6d2F2OTB1a3BKbm53Y0ZnUkVYMk51Vlg4bmRoMnVmeDVjTVR4NkwyRFFxZ0Jvdkp2YVVPaFUwX21PQQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[20]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi2wFBVV95cUxNbDlCbTdocnZLTGZQX3RCb1c2OG1wQl9CS2lHQ19XU2VTeUhadlNKRkw5dkJUT25pLWVXTGVwMm9oYk5LbFZRZ2duaTlxaVBSRDVveEZoQW5ZaEtZM09RUVVfeUlDaGJoc1cwcnh6MjJDaFZfNElXSi1fdnByd3lhSXQ3RlNxZ0U3QnBUcW40bXRiU1I0N3JoaWRYN3pqRVlwQXhtelozNkROWnR1cmFnXzhRanNqZUhnZlNVbHVfb2Fpdjc1TklmZWotU2ZXNG9PZC1LV2ZrY2xrRGc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[6]</a>. 국내에서는 창원특례시가 삼공퍼센트와 1조 894억 원 규모 80MW급 AI 데이터센터 투자 협약을 맺고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiVkFVX3lxTE9pMEhuWmhpZ29VZU5fMG1IdjZrcnQ1LWFIb0J1d25LSEM5aEloYTZIT2F1a1ExTWNyV1VfVWx1aGI4S0tuclFCTkVtVUwtQmFfNC1PY1Jn?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[26]</a>, 현대차그룹이 새만금 AI 데이터센터의 내년 3월 착공을 확정함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiakFVX3lxTE9pa2lrVk1DeFJlcUpxckt5dFlPOHc4SnZtMHNqUDVFc0pRRVM1Y29Palp3aDNTdmdtZnNlZm9rWm5FMHJDLVFNTlNaQzBzNV92bVBwZkNXWUtjb0s0STE2dlZHME1JXzZSc1HSAW9BVV95cUxNWTROcHVCOGcxdzZhcXo1aUNkNmxXa0V6Q3VxM3pxMnVrTnhXWUE0MU1SallTUlBUWThCSnVXWXZucHl6VEZYQmgtSm53aFJBVEpINy0xMHJYUm5EMGdYazNkU0ZSVGdqT1JGUXo1LXc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[24]</a>. 동시에 AWS가 한국을 피지컬 AI 거점으로 삼아 15개사 지원에 돌입하며 인프라 다변화를 견인함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMibEFVX3lxTFBGTHVucHRKVnJVb1hob1pHaWxKX0NhM0VYQkdhdFNCWUhwMC1oVS03V3g1SmpGYlFla0gtU3QxaFdIWmktWGxJWFlWMzU5dXVjbnFCZmFxNy1oTlBsQUhicl9yQlJXd0VlQThmNg?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[34]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiT0FVX3lxTFBpMzNIb2pBeUxxVEFFME56WXZTRU5QUllGeWl0QXVEdE0xeDBnRnZUUHdnQ1d1M1FRUnFmNkJSb1ZLOVBPSUhWOU5ZZUFwaEU?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[31]</a>.<BR><BR>AI 데이터센터와 첨단 반도체의 전력 수요 폭증이 국가 전력 계획의 재편과 초고압 배전 기자재 수주전을 촉발하는 흐름임. 대한민국 제12차 전력수급기본계획은 첨단 메가프로젝트를 반영해 2040년 최대 전력 수요를 27GW 상향된 165GW로 조정했으며, 이는 원전 17~19기 규모에 해당함 <a href='https://www.segye.com/newsView/20260820521179' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[30]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiiAFBVV95cUxQaENRTy05Y0N5QUtHYi13Mm1GaWg1Nl8yRi1ocm9yUGdpcjdueUNYcGhLbU1Fc0Itd0hmbnN6cFQtX2NMUkcwUXRFVGlrZVM3WWtRTmJiMEE4LUF1d1pTZnUtZGhaMWVCNHppVXpDVURFc2lrbTlZZEJXTE1XakF4Wi0ydGpGNnNK?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[17]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiXEFVX3lxTE9Fc0N4NXkzVU83WldDUlA0VzJFZzdHTDBPdDhadzczQTdrbGxfRVJseDh2X0t2THhMQ1lvdnB5RE02UVcwZGxlMkRvV3pzVzUyWVBjX2RhdWt3RXY5?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[29]</a>. 전력망 연계 지연에 직면한 아마존은 데이터센터 전용 초대형 천연가스 발전소 직접 건설에 돌입함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMimAFBVV95cUxPN012d2cxaWVIX0VjWXcycW5zaWdHY2hpRWhWX0x4RkVsTmhWVWVzUFpYUFhJeXlnSWxmVDFheE9hNDZwdnhRTUlTZlhka0h5VWNGMEZpOGUwMG5zVUIwZmo3QkxwNmZQdG84RjlqYWhacEZ4d0JTcUtwR21hTG9OdElPaW1KZWlxY3BBNHJyR2w1bVlzSFBwWA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[10]</a>. 기자재 부문에서는 가온전선이 미국 AI 클라우드 데이터센터에 2,000억 원 규모 버스덕트를 공급하기로 계약하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMickFVX3lxTE5Dand3RkUtOFpKRzVaM3FjSkEyTDVOQjltbGl5QWhqaHpKaTlqcG8yRTN4R01JcGUzcEhGYU9SV1pQbnQzQmQ4dFFXVUZ2Q0pWc1dYd2FzS0F1LWVqREVZVnRwTDVvOFNiQTl6MjJjd0h2QQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[15]</a> <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Gaon Cable to Supply ₩200 Billion (approximately $143.4 Million) in Busduct to US AI Cloud Data Center&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[3]</a>, LS일렉트릭과 LS전선은 CIGRE 2026에서 HVDC와 AIDC 통합 솔루션을 앞세워 유럽 전력망 시장 공략에 착수함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMia0FVX3lxTE5mdVd5RkstZ1hqR0tZT2ljbGYxRTE2SHI2UHBUV0luTlNsbExpTV9HemFSWV9fcml0UUxSZGd4cUx0bHNGcmJsblRRaDlIbVRGZV9vLWU3SEExam5UV0dlODA1bXpkSm9YSDJB?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[23]</a> <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Insight Europe Needs €584 Billion for Its Power Grids. LS Is Betting on HVDC and AI Data Centers&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a>. 아울러 슈나이더 일렉트릭은 차세대 고압 배전 솔루션(MCSeT with EvoPacT HVX)을 국내 데이터센터에 첫 공급함 <a href='https://www.aitimes.kr/news/articleView.html?idxno=41534' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[18]</a>.<BR><BR>초고밀도 랙 하중과 열설계전력(TDP) 급증을 제어하기 위해 물리적 건축의 강구조 전환과 냉각 및 광통신 하드웨어 혁신이 급물살을 타는 국면임. 현대제철은 한미글로벌과 맞손을 잡고 콘크리트를 대체하여 공기 단축과 고하중 지지가 가능한 데이터센터 전용 강구조(철골) 기술 협력에 전격 착수함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTE1mdFRDc0NGbXRINXZ5WGFrSFNJQmtsSEdpQkwzRFZRTDZWN2RUMjZxTGY2WlRES2V6UnpYUk5FRmFoeHd0U2pERS1ZNjg4LUVIdFpCSkxUSVFYakI0QktMVGNyeGs?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[27]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMidkFVX3lxTE1JM1o5Ty1KcThDY19FbHF2RjdoSUZPdUhyZTNUOC1fWE5qc2xHVzIwWXNzMVpJRkV3dHJOVW5wSDd5ajRvUnJrTzJvQzNWd1BwTno1VUhRVnFFUjdhVE9CVGNvM1daUDVuR0dOYlJlZ2ppSlVzdFE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[5]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMibkFVX3lxTE5ab0NCRFpPbmxDVkJjYl9LMTZkRC1YSVRVNmpRSGZZVGJabi02YWNtZ2tHZzI0X0pzdVJrWW9BWjRCTl9FRkFtMnpXV3lLVEM4Q2pVY1dLZmR4Z3F0UkFLSXE3U0ZjSWJkbEpCRnhR?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[14]</a>. 냉각 및 설비 제조에서는 유니시스가 레프로이드와 액체냉각 협력을 체결하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiwAFBVV95cUxPM0V2OWpxUlI3ZTRSZGd2RWFBNWJoU2s5cTZpeDc1RXhIUGNsWE1kTHg2d2xiNW04WTkydGZKQVFZWUhFYTdjQzVvNXRBNHNxbVFyc0JmR3Z1T3daWjNBZ05tZ1M4SmtfcjJ3UHl4ay04VkFOZ2lZYWVPWG1iWHFvajR5UkpaRkZ4ZWJCcmNMUGtTZXZtM2lnY01yUHRkdE9JaGZISDZ4c2c5emZtTkdkd2NNVkpsUnlzazBOQm1ScTM?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[12]</a>, 케이엔에스 자회사 은성FA가 미국 데이터센터용 버스덕트 자동화 설비를 수주함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiakFVX3lxTE1CZDF0TjBlUDNLMW9nSHJyYUJZdkxrX19kODRqVzVZN210OWxkNDdMQ0FGdnRSSTRBSm9yLUhjSWNTMUlnQlBFaW9MNHJ4QVNWTEhlMHp4MWZXd20zSi1OdDdvSjNITXZ6bWc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[25]</a>. 한편 142kW급 엔비디아 고전력 랙 도입으로 배선 병목이 심화됨에 따라 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiYEFVX3lxTE1fUy1BSUg4eXljUjYwR012ckFzXzhLRkw5UmI0bTlSVlZ6b3Azb0dUeTNWbnd1bzZ2eG5DMzZxRUIxeGZublc5ZVRGY2pPT3hlRlZ4SmxWTHlUSlh4dXBPUNIBZkFVX3lxTE94aVdjWWNSTHR6YUNHZnpaZmR6RUVERHh3S1FXeWw1YmxqYU1IM1pxbkZUdk5JZVVwbkh6VXVTWVcyOEdEOG5GUmRRTnozNXVBNWNXLUFPakhscDkxMU1Ec0lSN196UQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[32]</a>, 구리선의 한계를 넘어 빛으로 대용량 데이터를 처리하는 광 트랜시버와 8,000마일급 광섬유 백본망 구축이 핵심 하드웨어 트렌드로 안착함 <a href='https://www.mk.co.kr/news/business/12132570' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[33]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiYEFVX3lxTE50T055ckFJVWN5b3k3VkFJY1NDZ1hsM3JwMTc1MGUwS2RFN3d2WFJXMkd3VHU0OEpubWxqYVVRejJDVW5XMUFYQWE4SERWMjFvMVl0M0I3eWVncktyalBFbNIBZkFVX3lxTE5Xa2I0clY4X2J5SkV1UUpRcm8xNVRtX0ZnSmV2MWxTSDU0WHhwZGY3QVd4SzR1MHB3Mm9saUdFNENzNXBkaEpBZmh2YkFTNDlUTExBd2NXbHlKbHRnaDNQOEZhcTFWdw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[28]</a>.<BR><BR>전력망 과부하와 수자원 고갈에 따른 지역사회 반발이 입법 규제와 요금 차등화로 비화되는 가운데, 지속 가능한 공존 모델 확보가 핵심 과제로 부상함. 미국 테네시계곡개발청(TVA) 이사회는 주민 전기료 전가를 차단하기 위해 데이터센터 전용 전기요금 인상안을 공식 승인함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi5gFBVV95cUxNYVdhaFU3VVhUcVBBX2hGc3ItY2tNY1FPVzA2aE95aEtNUFNLSHJGVXJjY3Y1SmFlTDJ6amJsNXY0SlBfZXRLVkxhRzY5TVhkVWVmcTZPS2NqVTZ2SVRxSDFwUU5WcWJoOVZIRmJ1ZTA2TWM4YnJtSUw2ZEhZYlM1Yy1aYXJERHBtNzFUa3ZqS0ZKTmEybjlacmpIQ1VGMmJRMk44QU94aXNCSkVrc1VtbHJyc1pSTEVCYk0zazEwZ201b1ZvTmFTVHJwRllfNk9YeXRwa3dTWEc1OGowbmV4aG8ySVVDUQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[11]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMif0FVX3lxTE5hZEZySWYwLWNoQnF0SEVKNzNVSGNiTzlCZDRKQlpLWl9xck1kZm9pVnU3bVFxQTVMUVRObTkwRlJHaXhlSF85VUV5QWhhTF9aSXNuZ085a0ZkNGJ0YjJkRl9wUVExdDBqX2V2eFI4M21mSDFTOXZqMTBYMnRENE3SAYQBQVVfeXFMT0JOLUdRYm9zeGdNaGcwSlFsZE4tYjdFeTVtaUJPRjQxcENUbkdyQ2I1SDduUHEwUHVEQ2NLZ2ZTRi1vSUdVaTFPUTNEODk1M0NTVGtoV3VDZklScHZwN2JMUzlkV25IWE9vNjZKRHROT0JZNDJQUjZGV3UtYWx3WjJseHRS?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[21]</a>. 네바다주 나이 카운티는 수자원 고갈 유역 내 데이터센터 건설을 전면 금지하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiqwFBVV95cUxNS3VKdmFad3pVdWV5ZnV0dFhGTG1LWmQwdWRsYWczUkVUcV9ZSUZsVldIUmJ5cDBRR2lvd1hTWnR6MWQ5NVd0ZVdqYzRZWHNTNUw3X0s1RmNQUmVQY1NWMkNLaDdHbVM0U0lSY3pqMEwzd21HSjFwb000Y1M4b0pGdTdGNWUxUFJYQjBLUEh1Rks5YnhLRGNXUzVwcm5QcjlyZEJZVkw2SEN0Vzg?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[8]</a>, 텍사스 배스트럽 카운티에서는 세제 혜택 신청이 자진 철회됨 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi8AFBVV95cUxNT1BzejBtYUt1blY1UHZkeHBvbHNxZ3p3VUFpekJRYlNBbkVfOG1mSnpmTjhDVXU5dWdnUVlJNWh6OHdmdW5EU2tsX2xndzZFV3dOUW05Z1dobkRUZlc0eTRlNF9PVXY1cmpHZUw4M19jaEFQdmpfQVpzRldRY3c4NmR3UWdha255UjBUZlBkS3M1QkFPRFRFZ29vN3hqNWhqd3A4SlBnMUdJNVh1TW9QSHVhcGRtT2tWbmlwQnpFZm0xNk03NzF0aHhna2NDeC1RU3M5Q3RaTkN5SXpVM2J3SnhZb0RVYUxNRmo5TjZ4UDU?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[9]</a>. 국내에서도 포항 환경단체가 용수 및 폐수 대책 없는 AI 데이터센터 2단계 증설 중단을 요구하며 수자원 갈등이 촉발됨 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiT0FVX3lxTE9QNGh5SEVPWVZzYVVYNURkbFp2ZjFQMlJ3V2lQaVAzRXp2bXE2UXNIRWFUZzdYUEZHYnRKNnl4ck11WG1OelVZX0w1TVpwcGc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[16]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMic0FVX3lxTE9ZZkhDeVBuYlZYOFh1WjlOa1pRMlQ0NWVZbU0wY1lkTTBWeVJXNFJLUDk3M3h4MkRBRWxNOVRXakZpUEFWV09DLVZ2R1dYbUprSEtobzBKcmppYTNyeVlHU2YzeVhjcGp2UHpoaEtja2E1MVk?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[22]</a>. 이에 구글은 오클라호마 수자원 사업에 3,000만 달러 투입을 확정하며 대응에 나선 한편 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMirwFBVV95cUxNUEVYZEF3WHpIcWlnZXcxb2VUaW5TZ1NhN0JsSUtmSVNIVlg4ZzQ1LWxVMEV3TWI3X0NJbGxuX3dHa3l3OXZpQTdUM3NNVGY1NXdYZVBScEJzZWRSV1kxTFNMLTQ3Qjg3X3lGSll5S0NwLVhtdHRscWphQzl3QXljU3hZSXRZRWRIV2dTZTJZS0ZpV2FMbXQ4UUZxT2syWlAzNEJaLU5xSldRY3IzcDlV?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7;
